TL;DR: 本文重新评估了掩码扩散语言模型中的后验置信度重掩码方法（以WINO为例），发现在标准解码设置下该方法几乎没有带来收益，且会加剧多样性坍塌问题，其效果高度依赖具体设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型存在一个显著限制：一旦token被解掩码就无法再修改，容易受到早期采样错误的影响。为此，已有研究尝试扩展dLLM使其具备自纠正（重掩码）能力，其中一类方法以训练无关、后验的方式基于置信度进行重掩码。本文旨在重新审视这类方法的实际效果。

Method: 以WINO方法为代表的后验置信度重掩码方法，在标准解码设置（较短块长度）和非贪婪解码设置下进行实验评估，并与仅使用置信度解掩码的方法进行对比。

Result: 在标准解码设置下，后验置信度重掩码方法相比仅置信度解掩码几乎没有带来性能提升；在非贪婪解码中，虽然能部分缓解随机性引入的错误，但同时也加剧了置信度解掩码已有的多样性坍塌问题。

Conclusion: 后验置信度重掩码方法的收益高度依赖于具体解码设置，亟需一个更全面的评估框架来系统性地衡量其优劣。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (dLLMs) have recently emerged as a competitive alternative to autoregressive language models, with the promise of faster inference via parallel token generation. A notable limitation of the masked formulation, however, is that once a token has been unmasked it can no longer be revised, leaving dLLMs vulnerable to early sampling mistakes. To address this, a growing body of work has sought to extend masked dLLMs with self-correcting (remasking) capabilities. One appealing subset of these methods does so in a training-free, post-hoc manner based on token confidences, with encouraging early reported results. In this work, we revisit the empirical evaluation of a representative post-hoc remasking method, WINO [Hong et al., 2026], and find that under standard decoding settings (shorter block lengths) it brings little-to-no benefit over confidence-based unmasking alone [Wu et al., 2025]. Extending the evaluation to non-greedy decoding, we find that while confidence-based remasking can mitigate errors introduced by increased stochasticity to some extent, it also exacerbates the diversity collapse previously reported for confidence-based unmasking. Overall, our results show that the benefits of post-hoc confidence-based remasking are highly setting-dependent, underscoring the need for a more comprehensive evaluation framework.

</details>


### [179] [Multi-Rate Mixture of Experts for Accelerating Liquid Neural Network Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12240)
*Shilong Zong,Almuatazbellah Boker,Hoda Eldardiry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于液态神经网络的多速率混合专家框架（MR-MoE），通过多时间尺度专家分解、门控网络自适应专家选择以及特征级与时序级双重注意力机制，有效建模多变量时间序列中复杂的不规则采样与多尺度动态，在预测任务中取得了优于LSTM、单LNN和标准MoE的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列数据常呈现复杂的时间依赖、不规则采样以及跨多个时间尺度的异质动态，传统离散时间RNN（如LSTM）难以捕捉连续和不规则的时间行为，而标准LNN虽具备连续时间动态能力，但依赖单一动态系统，限制了其对异质时间模式的建模能力。

Method: 提出MR-MoE框架：1）多个LNN专家以不同时间尺度运行，分别捕捉快变动态和慢变趋势；2）门控网络根据输入条件实现自适应专家专业化；3）特征级注意力机制抑制噪声/无关变量，时序注意力机制聚焦信息丰富的历史状态。

Result: 在复杂多变量时间序列预测任务上，MR-MoE在AUROC和AUPRC指标上持续优于LSTM、单LNN和标准MoE基线，同时保持了良好的计算效率。

Conclusion: 结合连续时间动态、多尺度专家分解和自适应注意力机制能有效提升时间序列建模的性能。

Abstract: Multivariate time-series data often exhibit complex temporal dependencies, irregular sampling, and heterogeneous dynamics across multiple time scales, making accurate sequence modeling particularly challenging. Traditional recurrent neural networks (RNNs), such as Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, operate in discrete time and may struggle to effectively capture continuous and irregular temporal behaviors. Liquid Neural Networks (LNNs) address some of these limitations through continuous-time dynamics, but standard LNN architectures typically rely on a single dynamical system, limiting their ability to model heterogeneous temporal patterns. To address these challenges, we propose a Multi-Rate Mixture-of-Experts (MR-MoE) framework built on top of Liquid Neural Networks. In the proposed architecture, multiple LNN-based experts operate at distinct time scales, enabling the model to explicitly separate fast-changing dynamics from slow-evolving temporal trends. A gating network further enables adaptive expert specialization based on input conditions. In addition, we incorporate both feature-level and temporal attention mechanisms to improve robustness, interpretability, and long-range dependency modeling. Feature-level attention suppresses noisy or irrelevant variables, while temporal attention selectively focuses on informative historical states. We evaluate the proposed framework on a complex multivariate time-series prediction task and compare it against strong baselines, including LSTM, monolithic LNN, and standard MoE models. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed MR-MoE framework consistently achieves improved AUROC and AUPRC performance while maintaining favorable computational efficiency. These results highlight the effectiveness of combining continuous-time dynamics, multi-scale expert decomposition, and adaptive attention mechanisms for time-series modeling.

</details>


### [180] [Reinforcement Learning Disrupts Gradient-Based Adversarial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12251)
*Xinhai Zou,Chang Zhao,Alireza Aghabagherloo,Dave Singelée,Robin Degraeve,Bart Preneel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现强化学习（RL）训练通过破坏梯度结构使梯度攻击失效，其机制是RL作为隐式正则化器导致梯度方向不稳定且幅度减小；将RL与对抗训练结合（RL-adv）可形成梯度级+边界级双重防御，显著优于标准对抗训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于梯度的对抗攻击是DNN的主要威胁，现有防御主要关注对抗训练等边界级方法，但鲜有研究从破坏攻击者所依赖的梯度信息本身入手。本文探索RL训练是否能通过改变模型梯度结构来抵御梯度攻击。

Method: 使用策略梯度目标和epsilon-greedy探索训练图像分类器，在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、ImageNet-100上系统实验；通过损失景观可视化、静态/动态梯度指标、预测熵等进行机制分析；并将RL与对抗训练结合（RL-adv）形成双层防御。

Result: RL训练的模型显著破坏了梯度优化的对抗攻击；机制分析表明RL作为隐式正则化器，产生高度不稳定的梯度方向和较小的梯度幅度，使PGD每一步方向不可靠且幅度有限；RL-adv在所有攻击类型（PGD、AutoAttack、迁移攻击、查询攻击）上均优于SL-adv且优势显著。

Conclusion: RL诱导的梯度破坏是一种互补的鲁棒性机制，RL-adv实现梯度级+边界级的双层防御；未来可探索SL-RL混合训练策略以结合两者的优势。

Abstract: Gradient-based adversarial attacks remain a dominant threat to deep neural networks (DNNs), as they exploit gradient information to efficiently optimize adversarial perturbations. To address this, we investigate whether reinforcement learning (RL) training can disrupt the gradient structure used by attackers by training image classifiers with policy-gradient objectives and epsilon-greedy exploration. Through systematic experiments across CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNet-100 with multiple architectures, we find that RL-trained classifiers significantly disrupt gradient-based adversarial optimization. To explain this, we conduct a comprehensive mechanism analysis using loss landscape visualization, static and dynamic gradient indicators, and predictive entropy. Our analysis reveals that RL acts as an implicit regularizer, producing models with highly unstable gradient directions and smaller gradient magnitudes. This combination makes each PGD step both unreliable in direction and limited in magnitude, causing gradient-based attacks to fail within practical iteration budgets. We further show that combining RL with adversarial training (RL-adv) provides a dual-layer defense operating at two complementary levels: RL degrades gradient information available to attackers (gradient-level defense), while adversarial training strengthens decision boundaries (boundary-level defense). RL-adv achieves the highest robustness across all major attack types evaluated, including gradient-based (PGD, AutoAttack), transfer-based, and query-based attacks, outperforming SL-adv by a significant margin. These findings identify RL-induced gradient disruption as a complementary robustness mechanism and motivate future research on hybrid SL-RL training schedules that combine SL's efficiency with RL's gradient-regularization properties.

</details>


### [181] [Using Explainability as a Training-Time Reliability Signal for Efficient ECG Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12252)
*Veerendhra Kumar Dangeti,Xiao Gu,Ying Weng,Shreyank N Gowda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ERTS方法，利用解释质量（Grad-CAM注意力图的聚焦分数）作为训练信号，在ECG分类中过滤低质量样本、优先保留有意义的注意力样本，从而降低训练成本并提升macro-F1性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床时间序列分析中的深度神经网络训练计算成本高，许多医疗环境缺乏重复开发和部署的资源；现有Progressive Data Dropout方法依赖模型置信度筛选样本，但可能保留因噪声或模糊性导致的困难样本而非有用信号。

Method: 提出ERTS（Explainability-based Reliability Training Signal），在渐进式数据选择基础上，计算候选样本的Grad-CAM注意力图并推导聚焦分数，衡量模型预测是否由连贯且局部化的模式支持；低聚焦样本被过滤，高聚焦样本优先用于梯度更新。

Result: 在三个ECG数据集和多种骨干架构上，ERTS在降低有效训练成本的同时，持续提升了macro-F1指标。

Conclusion: 解释质量可作为实用的训练信号，在临床时间序列学习中同时提升训练效率和模型可靠性。

Abstract: Training deep neural networks for clinical time-series analysis is computationally demanding, yet many healthcare settings lack the resources required for repeated model development and deployment. This challenge is particularly evident in electrocardiogram classification, where large datasets and long training schedules make efficiency practically important. Progressive Data Dropout reduces training cost by excluding samples from gradient updates once they are learned, but it relies on model confidence and may retain samples that are difficult due to noise or ambiguity rather than useful signal. In this work, we introduce ERTS, an explainability-based reliability training signal for efficient ECG classification. ERTS uses explanation quality during training to distinguish between informative and unreliable uncertainty. Building on progressive data selection, we compute Grad-CAM attention maps for candidate samples and derive a focus score that measures whether model predictions are supported by coherent and localised patterns. Samples with low focus are filtered out, while those with meaningful attention are prioritised for gradient updates. We evaluate ERTS across three ECG datasets and multiple backbone architectures, showing consistent improvements in macro-F1 alongside reduced effective training cost. These results suggest that explanation quality can serve as a practical signal for improving both efficiency and reliability in clinical time-series learning. Code will be released.

</details>


### [182] [Finding Multiple Interpretations in Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12277)
*Matthew Chak,Paul Anderson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种方法，用于在损失/准确率度量下找到表现相似但上下文感知特征高度不同的模型集合。在METABRIC数据集上的实验表明，该方法能在不牺牲性能的前提下，找到具有显著不同基因表达模式的多个模型。该方法对于分析模型的全局特征以提取对潜在现象的洞察具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法倾向于找到性能相似的模型，但忽略了这些模型在上下文感知特征（如基因表达模式）上的差异。作者希望能够在保持模型性能的同时，发现具有不同内在特征的模型，从而更好地分析潜在现象。

Method: 提出一种用于寻找性能相似但上下文感知特征高度不同的模型集合的方法。在METABRIC数据集上进行实验验证，通过与对照方法比较，证明该方法能找到基因表达模式显著不同的模型。

Result: 在METABRIC数据集上，该方法成功找到了多个与对照方法相比具有高度不同基因表达模式的模型，且未产生性能损失。

Conclusion: 该方法对于需要在保持性能的同时分析模型全局特征、提取潜在现象洞察的场景具有重要意义。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose an approach to finding sets of similar-performing models (in terms of loss/accuracy measurements) with highly different context-aware characteristics. Through experiments on the METABRIC dataset, we show that the proposed method finds multiple models with highly different gene expressions than those found by the control methodology without performance penalties. We argue that the proposed methodology is important whenever one aims to analyze any global characteristic of a model to extract insight into the underlying phenomenon being studied.

</details>


### [183] [Holding the FP8 Quality Ceiling at 8-Bit Weights and Activations: INT8 and GGUF Post-Training Quantization of Ideogram 4.0 for Consumer GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12280)
*Deep Gandhi,Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对Ideogram 4.0（9.3B参数的流匹配扩散Transformer）进行后训练量化，在缺乏FP8张量核心的Ampere RTX 3090 GPU上实现INT8 W8A8量化。INT8方案（每通道权重、每token动态激活、SmoothQuant、混合精度保护高脆弱层）保持了FP8的质量上限，在200提示词基准上INT8与FP8的配对bootstrap CI包含零，INT8相比NF4提升+1.9 CLIP。GGUF Q4_K在同等磁盘大小下优于NF4，是质量-内存帕累托最优。论文还首次对该模型类别进行了逐类别OCR分析，并通过消融实验确定FFN下投影保护是主导质量杠杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练量化能让大规模文本到图像扩散Transformer在消费级GPU上运行，但硬件特定的质量-速度权衡很少被直接测量。本文旨在填补这一空白，针对缺乏FP8张量核心的Ampere架构GPU，系统评估INT8量化方案的实际效果。

Method: 对Ideogram 4.0（9.3B流匹配DiT，含Qwen3-VL-8B编码器）进行INT8 W8A8量化：每通道权重量化、每token动态激活量化、SmoothQuant、混合精度保护高脆弱层集。使用200提示词基准进行配对bootstrap置信区间评估，进行逐类别OCR分析，以及消融实验和GGUF Q4_K量化对比。

Result: INT8与FP8质量持平（CI包含零），INT8相比NF4提升+1.9 CLIP（95% CI [+1.21,+2.64]）。GGUF Q4_K优于同等大小的NF4，是帕累托最优。消融实验表明FFN下投影保护是主导质量杠杆。INT8权重体积与FP8相当，在Ampere上需融合INT8内核才能提速。

Conclusion: 在Ampere GPU上，INT8 W8A8量化可在不损失质量的前提下替代FP8，但速度优势需等 fused INT8 kernel实现。GGUF Q4_K是质量-内存权衡的最佳选择。FFN下投影层对量化质量最为关键。

Abstract: Post-training quantization lets large text-to-image diffusion transformers run on consumer GPUs, yet the hardware-specific trade-offs are seldom measured directly. We quantize Ideogram 4.0 - a 9.3B flow-matching diffusion transformer (DiT), shipped as two separate-weight copies of a single-stream 34-layer backbone for classifier-free guidance and conditioned by a Qwen3-VL-8B encoder - for Ampere RTX 3090 GPUs, which lack FP8 tensor cores. Our INT8 W8A8 recipe (per-channel weights, per-token dynamic activations, SmoothQuant, and mixed-precision protection of a small high-fragility layer set) holds the FP8 quality ceiling: on a 200-prompt benchmark the paired same-seed bootstrap CI for INT8-FP8 includes zero on both Pick and CLIP, while INT8 improves on NF4 by $+1.9$ CLIP (95% CI $[+1.21,+2.64]$, excluding zero). A per-category OCR analysis, to our knowledge unreported for this model class, confirms text legibility is preserved, and an ablation isolates protection of the FFN down-projections as the dominant quality lever. Our GGUF Q4_K quantization beats NF4 at equal on-disk size and is the Pareto winner on the quality-memory frontier, with paired confidence intervals excluding zero (Q8_0 is quality neutral). Finally, we characterize where 8-bit quantization helps and where it does not: INT8's weights match FP8's footprint rather than shrink it, so a speed gain on Ampere awaits a fused INT8 kernel.

</details>


### [184] [The Standard Interpretable Model: A general theory of interpretable machine learning to deductively design interpretable methods using Lagrangian mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12289)
*Pietro Barbiero,Giovanni De Felice,Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga,Francesco Giannini,Filippo Bonchi,Mateja Jamnik,Giuseppe Marra,Ruggero Noris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于拉格朗日力学的标准可解释模型（SIM）理论框架，通过公理化推导可解释性对称性与约束条件，将可解释方法的设计从经验驱动转向演绎驱动，统一了碎片化的可解释性研究领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可解释性研究缺乏通用理论来演绎地设计可解释方法，导致文献碎片化和评估协议不一致，亟需一个统一的理论基础来弥合理论与方法之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出标准可解释模型（SIM），以一组前提概括目标用户对可解释性的需求，从中系统性地推导可解释性对称性及对应约束，构建拉格朗日量，其最小值对应最优可解释模型；通过更新黑箱模型参数或编译约束到可解释架构两种方式达到最小值。

Result: SIM能够识别并解决现有方法（包括传统、概念基和机制可解释性）的局限性，突出未充分探索的研究方向，并为核心编程接口的设计提供指导。

Conclusion: SIM不仅是研究方法，其演绎性质为可解释性课程提供了教学基础，可能改变该学科长期碎片化的科学共同体视角。

Abstract: As Artificial Intelligence models grow in complexity, interpretability has become an indispensable tool for understanding, debugging, and controlling their computations. However, interpretability lacks general theories to deductively design interpretable methods. This gap between theories and methods results in a fragmented literature and inconsistent evaluation protocols.
  To fill this gap, we introduce the Standard Interpretable Model (SIM), a general theory grounded in Lagrangian mechanics that enables the deductive design of interpretable methods. Specifically, the SIM summarises, in a set of premises, what interpretability is for a target user. From these premises, the SIM systematically derives interpretability symmetries and corresponding constraints, which shape the landscape of a Lagrangian whose minima correspond to optimal interpretable models. To reach the minima, one can either update the parameter values of an opaque model to make it more interpretable or compile constraints into an interpretable architecture.
  We empirically show that the SIM identifies and solves limitations of existing methods (including traditional, concept-based, and mechanistic interpretability), highlights underexplored research directions, and informs the design of core programming interfaces. Beyond being a research method, the deductive nature of the SIM offers pedagogical grounding for interpretability curricula and may shift the scientific community's perspective of a discipline that has long been fragmented.

</details>


### [185] [Harness In-Context Operator Learning with Chain of Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12318)
*Minghui Yang,Ling Guo,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Chain of Operators (CHOP)框架，通过构建包含显式初等变换和冻结ICON的操作链，使预训练的ICON无需参数更新即可泛化到分布外算子任务，在标量守恒律和平均场控制问题上降低了推理误差，且操作链可跨PDE族泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子（如ICON）在函数空间映射中表现良好，但泛化到分布外（OOD）算子任务时性能下降，需要微调或重训练。受大语言模型提示工程启发，作者希望在不更新参数的前提下，让冻结的ICON适应OOD算子任务。

Method: 提出Chain of Operators (CHOP)框架，将冻结的ICON与显式初等变换（如缩放、平移等可解析表达的操作）串联成一个操作链，通过组合这些可解释的闭式变换来适应不同的OOD算子任务，无需更新ICON参数。

Result: 在标量守恒律和平均场控制问题上的实验表明，CHOP相比直接使用ICON评估，显著降低了相对推理误差。同时，在一个PDE族上构建的操作链能够泛化到另一个不同的PDE族。

Conclusion: CHOP框架验证了通过可解释的显式变换链来增强预训练神经算子的OOD泛化能力是有效的，且不同动力系统之间可能存在共享的底层机制。

Abstract: Neural operators approximate mappings between function spaces, but often generalize poorly to other operators and usually require fine-tuning or retraining. In-Context Operator Networks (ICON) addresses this issue by prompting the model with numerical context so that the model learns specific operators from prompts and adapt to different operators without fine-tuning. However, ICON may still fail to generalize to out-of-distribution (OOD) operator tasks. Inpired by the success of harness engineering of Large Language models (LLMs), we introduce Chain of Operators (CHOP), a framework that harness a frozen ICON to OOD operator tasks without updating its parameters. Specifically, CHOP constructs a chain of operators consisting of explicit elementary transformations and the frozen ICON. Experiments on a scalar conservation law and a mean-field control problem show that CHOP reduces relative inference error over direct ICON evaluation, while each operator in the chain remains interpretable and in closed form. A chain constructed on one PDE family further generalizes to a different family, indicating shared mechanisms across harness systems.

</details>


### [186] [Fourier Features Let Agents Learn High Precision Policies with Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12334)
*Balázs Gyenes,Emiliyan Gospodinov,Jan Frieling,Enrico Krohmer,Nicolas Schreiber,Xiaogang Jia,Niklas Freymuth,Gerhard Neumann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将点云从笛卡尔空间映射到高维傅里叶空间，使点云编码器能直接访问高频特征，从而提升基于点云的模仿学习策略在精细操作任务中的性能。实验在RoboCasa、ManiSkill3基准测试及真实机器人上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB-only策略因深度模糊和透视尺度问题难以实现高精度空间推理；而直接利用3D信息（如点云）的策略虽具有几何先验优势，但性能高度依赖具体任务。作者假设这种差异源于神经网络对低频函数的谱偏置（spectral bias），尤其是在处理缓慢变化的笛卡尔特征时更为明显。

Method: 将点云从笛卡尔空间映射到高维傅里叶空间，使点云编码器能直接访问高频特征。该方法简单易用，可适配多种编码器架构。

Result: 在RoboCasa和ManiSkill3基准测试以及真实机器人平台上，傅里叶特征在多种编码器架构和超参数设置下均显著优于笛卡尔特征，展现出良好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 傅里叶特征使策略能比笛卡尔特征更有效地利用几何细节，有望成为基于点云的模仿学习的通用工具。

Abstract: High-precision robotic manipulation requires fine-grained spatial reasoning that is often difficult to achieve with RGB-only policies due to depth ambiguity and perspective scale issues. Policies that leverage 3D information directly, such as those based on point clouds, offer a stronger geometric prior over purely image-based ones, yet their performance remains highly task-dependent. We hypothesize that this discrepancy may be due to the spectral bias of neural networks towards learning low frequency functions, which especially affects architectures conditioned on slow-moving Cartesian features. We thus propose to map point clouds from Cartesian space into high-dimensional Fourier space, effectively equipping the point cloud encoder with direct access to high-frequency features. We experimentally validate the use of Fourier features on challenging manipulation tasks from the RoboCasa and ManiSkill3 benchmarks and on a real robot setup. Despite their simplicity, we find that Fourier features provide significant benefits across diverse encoder architectures and benchmarks and are robust across hyperparameters. Our results indicate that Fourier features let policies leverage geometric details more effectively than Cartesian features, showing their potential as a general-purpose tool for point cloud-based imitation learning. We provide source code and videos on our project page: https://fourier-il.github.io/fourier-il

</details>


### [187] [Claw-SWE-Bench: A Benchmark for Evaluating OpenClaw-style Agent Harnesses on Coding Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12344)
*Mengyu Zheng,Kai Han,Boxun Li,Haiyang Xu,Yuchuan Tian,Wei He,Hang Zhou,Jianyuan Guo,Hailin Hu,Lin Ma,Chao Xu,Guohao Dai,Lixue Xia,Yunchao Wei,Yunhe Wang,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Claw-SWE-Bench，一个多语言、适配器协议的SWE-bench风格基准测试，使不同通用智能体（如OpenClaw）在编码任务上可公平比较。基准包含350个问题实例（8种语言、43个仓库），并验证了适配器设计对智能体编码性能的关键影响（从19.1%提升至73.4%），模型选择和智能体架构对Pass@1影响分别为29.4pp和27.4pp，同时将成本和适配器设计纳入评估核心维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用智能体（如OpenClaw）作为自主工具用户越来越普遍，但它们在SWE-bench框架下的编码能力难以评测，因为通用智能体本身不满足SWE-bench要求的干净Docker工作空间、补丁和预测合约。因此需要设计一个适配协议和基准测试，使异构智能体可在公平设置下进行比较。

Method: 提出Claw-SWE-Bench基准测试和适配器协议，包括固定提示、运行时预算、工作空间合约、补丁提取过程和评估器。基准包含从SWE-bench-Multilingual和SWE-bench-Verified-Mini经未来提交清理后获得的350个GitHub问题解决实例（8种语言、43个仓库）。还发布了80实例的Lite版，通过成本感知和排名感知程序从17个校准列中选择。定义了两种适配器：最小direct-diff适配器和完整适配器。

Result: 在完整基准上，OpenClaw使用最小direct-diff适配器仅得19.1% Pass@1，而完整适配器在相同GLM 5.1骨干下达到73.4%。跨OpenClaw×9模型扫描和5-claw×2模型扫描，模型选择改变Pass@1达29.4个百分点，固定模型下智能体选择改变27.4个百分点。系统间准确率相近时总API成本可能差异显著。

Conclusion: Claw-SWE-Bench将适配器设计和成本核算作为SWE风格编码智能体评估的一级维度，提供了完整基准和低成本参考集用于可重复比较。适配器设计对发挥通用智能体编码能力至关重要。

Abstract: General-purpose agents such as OpenClaw are increasingly used as autonomous tool users, but their coding ability is difficult to measure under SWE-bench: a generic agent does not by itself satisfy the clean Docker workspace, patch, and prediction contract required for scoring. We introduce Claw-SWE-Bench, a multilingual SWE-bench-style benchmark and adapter protocol that makes heterogeneous agent harnesses, or claws, comparable under fair settings including a fixed prompt, runtime budget, workspace contract, patch extraction procedure, and evaluator. The full benchmark contains 350 GitHub issue-resolution instances across 8 languages and 43 repositories, drawn from SWE-bench-Multilingual and SWE-bench-Verified-Mini after future-commit cleanup. We also release Claw-SWE-Bench Lite for faster validation, which is an 80-instance subset selected by a cost-aware, rank-aware procedure over 17 calibration columns. On the full benchmark, OpenClaw with a minimal direct-diff adapter scores only $19.1\%$ Pass@1, whereas the full adapter reaches $73.4\%$ with the same GLM 5.1 backbone, showing that adapter design is essential for enabling OpenClaw-style harnesses to perform coding tasks effectively. Across an OpenClaw $\times$ nine-model sweep and a five-claw $\times$ two-model sweep, model choice changes Pass@1 by $29.4$ pp and harness choice by $27.4$ pp under fixed models; systems with similar accuracy can differ substantially in total API cost. Claw-SWE-Bench therefore treats harness and cost accounting as first-class axes of SWE-style coding-agent evaluation, providing both a full benchmark and a low-cost reference set for reproducible comparison. The data is available at https://github.com/opensquilla/claw-swe-bench and https://huggingface.co/datasets/TokenRhythm/Claw-SWE-Bench.

</details>


### [188] [Anatomy of Post-Training: Using Interpretability to Characterize Data and Shape the Learning Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12360)
*Leon Bergen,Usha Bhalla,Sidharth Baskaran,Max Loeffler,Raphael Sarfati,Dhruvil Gala,Ryan Panwar,Santiago Aranguri,Thomas Fel,Atticus Geiger,Matthew Kowal,Siddharth Boppana,Daniel Balsam,Owen Lewis,Jack Merullo,Thomas McGrath,Ekdeep Singh Lubana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可解释性的数据驱动后训练流水线，通过在概念层面检查偏好数据集并明确区分偏好与非偏好生成中的潜在概念，从而取代传统标量奖励优化，减少过风格化和谄媚等不良行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型后训练主要依赖标量奖励优化，这种方式难以让从业者了解数据实际教会了模型什么，导致模型学会虚假相关性并产生不良行为（如过风格化、谄媚）。作者希望能在优化前检查偏好数据集，在概念层面决定哪些行为应被允许学习。

Method: 提出一个数据驱动的后训练流水线：利用可解释性协议建立统计假设，揭示偏好与非偏好生成之间的潜在概念差异；将这些概念显式化用于细粒度用户反馈；统一了多种基于可解释性的训练协议，通过特征或数据干预来塑造奖励。

Result: 实验表明该流水线能够诊断现有偏好数据中的不良信号，减轻偏离目标的学习，并能帮助放大或塑造期望属性（如安全防护、模型个性）。

Conclusion: 可解释性能够将后训练从优化不透明的代理奖励转变为审计和塑造学习信号本身的过程。

Abstract: Language-model post-training is the main stage at which model behavior is shaped, yet it still largely involves optimization of scalar rewards that summarize diverse desiderata. This abstraction gives practitioners little visibility into what their data actually teaches models, allowing spurious correlations to be learned by a model and inducing undesirable behaviors such as over-stylization and sycophancy. To address this problem, we ask: can we inspect a preference dataset before optimization and decide, at the level of concepts, which behaviors a model should be allowed to learn? Motivated by this, we introduce a data-centric post-training pipeline that uses interpretability protocols to develop statistical hypotheses for the latent concepts separating preferred from dispreferred generations, making them explicit for fine-grained user feedback. Building on this view, we unify several interpretability-based training protocols as ways of shaping rewards via feature or data interventions. Empirically, we show that our pipeline diagnoses undesirable signals in existing preference data, mitigates off-target learning, and can also help amplify or shape desired properties such as safeguards and model personality. More broadly, our results suggest that interpretability can turn post-training from optimizing opaque proxy rewards into a process of auditing and sculpting the learning signal itself.

</details>


### [189] [Latent World Recovery for Multimodal Learning with Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12362)
*Hui Wang,Tianyu Ren,Joseph Butler,Christopher Baker,Karen Rafferty,Simon McDade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Latent World Recovery (LWR)框架，通过邻域潜在对齐和可用性感知融合，在不完整多模态数据下实现鲁棒预测，无需显式插补缺失模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物科学应用中，异构模态数据往往部分缺失，现有方法需要固定模态集或进行模态插补，存在误差传播问题，亟需一种能处理任意可用模态组合的统一学习框架。

Method: LWR包含两个核心思想：(i) 将不同模态的特定嵌入在共享潜在空间中对齐；(ii) 仅在训练和推理时利用实际可用的模态嵌入进行融合，构建统一表示。通过邻域基潜在对齐和可用性感知融合实现，避免显式重构缺失模态。

Result: 在真实世界不完整多组学基准上评估，在癌症表型分类和生存预测等下游任务上表现有效。

Conclusion: LWR通过将各模态视为潜在状态的局部感知，进行可用性感知表示学习，避免了缺失模态重构带来的误差传播，为不完整多模态学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: We study multimodal learning under missing modalities, with particular motivation from bioscience applications in which heterogeneous modalities are often only partially available when decisions need to be made. We propose Latent World Recovery (LWR), a framework built on two key ideas: (i) modality-specific embeddings from different modalities are aligned in a shared latent space, and (ii) a unified representation is constructed by fusing only the embeddings of the modalities that are actually available at both training and inference time. Rather than imputing missing modalities or requiring a fixed modality set, LWR treats each modality as a partial perception of an underlying latent state and performs availability-aware representation learning directly from the observed modalities. This combination of neighbor-based latent alignment and availability-aware modality fusion enables robust multimodal prediction under partial observation, while avoiding error propagation from explicit reconstruction of missing modalities. We evaluate the proposed framework on real-world incomplete multi-omics benchmarks and demonstrate that it provides an effective approach to downstream tasks such as cancer phenotype classification and survival prediction.

</details>


### [190] [On Subquadratic Architectures: From Applications to Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12364)
*Anamaria-Roberta Hartl,Levente Zólyomi,David Stap,Pieter-Jan Hoedt,Niklas Schmidinger,Lukas Hauzenberger,Sebastian Böck,Günter Klambauer,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了xLSTM、Mamba-2和Gated DeltaNet三种次二次架构，在代码模型预训练、代码模型蒸馏和时间序列基础模型预训练三个复杂任务上，xLSTM表现最优。其优势源于更灵活稳定的门控机制带来的状态跟踪和记忆动态能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的二次注意力计算成本高昂，次二次架构虽可扩展，但哪种设计最有效尚不明确，需要系统比较分析。

Method: 在代码模型预训练、大模型蒸馏和时间序列基础模型三个复杂依赖任务上，系统比较xLSTM、Mamba-2和Gated DeltaNet，并通过统一公式分析架构机制，结合合成长度泛化任务验证。

Result: xLSTM在所有三个任务上取得最佳性能，其门控机制实现了更灵活稳定的记忆校正，在合成长度泛化任务上也得到验证。

Conclusion: xLSTM在复杂任务上的优势源于其稳健的状态跟踪和累积能力，门控方案是关键机制。

Abstract: Transformers dominate modern sequence modeling, but their quadratic attention incurs substantial computational cost. Subquadratic architectures offer a scalable alternative. However, it remains unclear which designs yield the most effective sequence models. We compare three leading approaches: xLSTM, Mamba-2, and Gated DeltaNet. We evaluate these models on tasks with complex dependencies: (1) code-model pre-training, (2) distillation of code models from large language models, and (3) pre-training of time-series foundation models. Across these settings, xLSTM delivers the strongest overall performance. To explain xLSTM's advantage, we present a unified formulation and analyze the underlying architectural mechanisms, focusing on state tracking and memory dynamics. Our results show that xLSTM enables more flexible and stable memory correction via its gating scheme. We corroborate these findings on controlled synthetic length-generalization tasks. Overall, our findings indicate that xLSTM's gains on complex tasks stem from robust state tracking and accumulation.

</details>


### [191] [Breaking Entropy Bounds: Accelerating RL Training via MTP with Rejection Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12370)
*Yucheng Li,Huiqiang Jiang,Yang Xu,Jianxin Yang,Yi Zhang,Yizhong Cao,Yuhao Shen,Fan Zhou,Rui Men,Jianwei Zhang,An Yang,Bowen Yu,Bo Zheng,Fei Huang,Junyang Lin,Dayiheng Liu,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多token预测（MTP）在LLM强化学习后训练中的应用，发现MTP接受率受模型熵波动限制，并提出端到端TV损失函数和拒绝采样策略，实现高达1.8倍的异步RL训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL训练中rollout阶段是关键瓶颈，MTP虽可通过推测解码加速，但现有方法在RL训练中接受率下降严重，加速效果有限。

Method: 1) 揭示MTP接受率与模型熵的负线性关系；2) 提出端到端TV损失函数直接优化多步拒绝采样接受率；3) 采用拒绝采样替代贪心草稿采样；4) 验证RL前MTP训练即可保持稳定加速，无需在线更新。

Result: 接受率提升~10%，达到最高95%；推理吞吐量额外提升25%；数学推理、代码生成和智能体任务上均有效；在Qwen3.5/3.6/3.7模型上实现最高1.8倍端到端加速。

Conclusion: 提出Bebop框架，通过端到端TV损失和拒绝采样策略有效解决了RL训练中MTP加速退化问题，实现了稳定高效的加速效果，且无需在线更新MTP。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a key component in modern large language models, yet the rollout stage remains the key bottleneck in RL training pipelines. Although Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) offers a natural solution to accelerate rollouts through speculative decoding, many studies have observed that MTP acceptance rates degrade significantly during RL training, leading to limited speedup performance. To address this bottleneck, we present Bebop, a systematic study of MTP in LLM post-training, and offer practical recipes to integrate MTP into large-scale RL pipelines. First, we reveal that the MTP acceptance rate is fundamentally bounded by the fluctuation of model entropy, which demonstrates a clear negative linear relationship with the rise of entropy in the RL stage. Second, we show that probabilistic rejection sampling largely alleviates the disturbance introduced by entropy in RL compared to greedy draft sampling. We further identify that the conventional MTP training objectives (cross-entropy or KL) are suboptimal in such settings, and therefore we propose a novel end-to-end TV loss that directly optimizes multi-step rejection sampling acceptance rate, yielding ~10% acceptance rate improvements, achieving up to 95% acceptance rates and up to 25% extra inference throughput gains across mathematical reasoning, code generation, and agentic tasks. Third, we test various online MTP training strategies during RL and show that pre-RL MTP training with e2e TV loss and rejection sampling achieves a consistent acceptance rate and speedup throughout the entire RL, eliminating the need for costly online MTP updating. We provide extensive experiments and analysis that validate our findings. Experimental results show our method achieves up to 1.8x end-to-end acceleration in async RL training of Qwen3.5, Qwen3.6, and Qwen3.7 models.

</details>


### [192] [APPO: Agentic Procedural Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12384)
*Xucong Wang,Ziyu Ma,Yong Wang,Yuxiang Ji,Shidong Yang,Guanhua Chen,Pengkun Wang,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Agentic Procedural Policy Optimization (APPO)，通过细粒度决策点分支与信用分配，提升多轮工具使用场景下的强化学习效果，在13个基准上平均提升近4个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agentic RL方法在粗粒度单元（如工具调用边界）上进行信用分配，难以识别影响下游结果的中间决策点。

Method: APPO方法：1) 使用结合token不确定性与策略诱导似然增益的分支分数(Branching Score)选择分支位置；2) 引入过程级优势缩放(procedure-level advantage scaling)实现细粒度信用分配。

Result: 在13个benchmark上，APPO一致性地提升强agentic RL基线近4个点，同时保持高效工具调用和可解释性。

Conclusion: 细粒度决策点分支与信用分配能有效提升agentic RL在复杂多轮交互中的表现。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic Reinforcement Learning (RL) have substantially improved the multi-turn tool-use capabilities of large language model agents. However, most existing methods assign credit over coarse heuristic units, such as tool-call boundaries or fixed workflows, making it difficult to identify which intermediate decisions influence downstream outcomes. In this work, we study agentic RL from two perspectives: \textit{where to branch and how to assign credit after branching}. Our pilot analysis shows that influential decision points are broadly distributed throughout the generated sequence rather than concentrated at tool calls, while token entropy alone does not reliably reflect their impact on final outcomes. Motivated by these observations, we propose \textbf{Agentic Procedural Policy Optimization (APPO)}, which shifts branching and credit assignment from coarse interaction units to fine-grained decision points in the sequence. APPO selects branching locations using a Branching Score that combines token uncertainty with policy-induced likelihood gains of subsequent continuations, enabling more targeted exploration while filtering out spurious high-entropy positions. It further introduces procedure-level advantage scaling to better distribute credit across branched rollouts. Experiments on 13 benchmarks show that APPO consistently improves strong agentic RL baselines by nearly 4 points, while keeping efficient tool-calls and maintaining behavior interpretability.

</details>


### [193] [ATLAS: Active Theory Learning for Automated Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12386)
*Noémi Éltető,Nathaniel D. Daw,Kimberly L. Stachenfeld,Kevin J. Miller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ATLAS（主动理论学习的自动化科学框架），通过主动学习迭代生成可解释的行为模型（解耦循环神经网络）并设计区分性实验，在强化学习代理恢复任务中相比随机实验实现5-10倍样本效率提升，经专家设计实验验证，展示了在认知科学等领域加速机制发现的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在认知科学中，通过机制建模推进科学理解需要提出正确的实验问题以产生最大化信息量的数据，但这一过程通常依赖人工。为自动化这一探索过程，作者引入ATLAS框架，实现数据驱动的可解释行为模型发现。

Method: ATLAS采用主动学习循环：1）生成机制假设——实例化为多样化的稀疏神经网络集成（解耦RNN）；2）设计能够最优区分不同假设的实验。在赌博任务中测试了恢复强化学习代理行为的能力。

Result: 在所有评估机制建模的指标（行为、结构、计算相似性）上，ATLAS相比随机实验实现5-10倍样本效率提升，性能进一步通过与文献中专家设计实验的对比得到验证。

Conclusion: ATLAS展示了在认知科学及其他依赖发现机制模型的科学领域中加速产生人类可解释洞察的潜力。

Abstract: Advancing scientific understanding through mechanistic modeling requires posing the right experimental questions to yield maximally informative data. To automate this pursuit within cognitive science, we introduce ATLAS (Active Theory Learning for Automated Science), an active learning framework for the data-driven discovery of interpretable behavioral models. ATLAS iterates between generating mechanistic hypotheses--instantiated as a diverse ensemble of sparse neural networks (Disentangled RNNs)--and designing experiments that optimally distinguish between them. We test this approach on the problem of recovering reinforcement learning agents from their behavior in bandit tasks. ATLAS designs varied sequences of qualitatively novel experiments with temporal structure tailored to underlying agent characteristics. The models trained on these experiments are evaluated against a comprehensive set of metrics for mechanistic modeling that capture behavioral, structural, and computational similarity. ATLAS achieves a 5-10x improvement in sample efficiency across all metrics compared to random experimentation, and its performance is further validated against expert-designed experiments derived from literature. These in silico results showcase ATLAS's potential to accelerate human-interpretable insights in cognitive science and other domains where scientific inquiry relies on discovering mechanistic models.

</details>


### [194] [Redesign Mixture-of-Experts Routers with Manifold Power Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12397)
*Songhao Wu,Ang Lv,Ruobing Xie,Yankai Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过流形幂迭代（MPI）方法将MoE路由器的每一行与对应专家的主奇异方向对齐，使路由矩阵更好地编码专家信息，从而提升MoE模型效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型中，路由器矩阵的行作为专家代理，通过计算与输入token的相似度来决定激活哪些专家。理想情况下，每一行应编码对应专家矩阵的代表性向量，使得点积能更好反映token-专家亲和度，但目前缺乏强制这种浓缩的设计原则。

Method: 提出将路由器每一行与关联专家的主奇异方向对齐，并设计了流形幂迭代（MPI）方法，采用"幂迭代后回缩"范式：先对路由器权重执行幂迭代步骤，再通过回缩施加范数约束以保障效率与稳定性。

Result: 理论证明了MPI驱动路由器行收敛到对应专家的主奇异方向；在1B到11B参数的MoE模型预训练实验中，证实该对齐方式能带来更有效的MoE模型。

Conclusion: 通过将路由器行与专家主奇异方向对齐，可以显著提升MoE路由器的表征能力，从而训练出更高效的混合专家模型。

Abstract: Router is the cornerstone component to the Mixture-of-Experts models. Serving as expert proxies, the rows of the router matrix compute their similarity to the MoE inputs to determine which subset of experts is activated. Ideally, each router row is designed to encode the expert matrix into this representative vector, such that its dot-product with token can better reflect token-expert affinity. However, there exists no design principles to enforce this condensation. In this paper, we propose to align each router row with the principal singular direction of the associated expert, as this direction provides the most expressive mathematical description of a matrix. Based on this principle, we propose a router redesign with Manifold Power Iteration (MPI). Specifically, it introduces a "Power-then-Retract" paradigm, where a power iteration step is performed on the router weights, followed by a retraction to impose a norm constraint to ensure both efficiency and stability. Theoretically, we show that MPI drives router rows to converge toward the principal singular directions of associated experts. Empirically, we pretrain MoE model across scales from 1B to 11B parameters to confirm that this alignment facilitates more effective MoE models.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [195] [The Geometry of Admissible Short Selling in Discrete-Time Stochastic Portfolio Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11191)
*Jilong Xu,Xiaojun Cui*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了随机资产组合理论的几何框架，将函数生成从长期-only投资组合推广到可破产保护的多空投资组合，证明了伪套利由生成函数的凹性刻画，并建立了卖空机制与生成势的最大凹延拓负性之间的等价关系，引入重心缩放变换构造可控卖空策略，发现了几何相变现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散时间随机资产组合理论（SPT）中，函数生成的工作主要局限于定义在整个单位单纯形上的长期-only投资组合，缺乏对更广泛的多空投资组合的几何框架研究。

Method: 1. 将函数生成的几何框架扩展到定义在局部市场状态空间上的可破产保护多空投资组合。2. 建立伪套利与生成函数凹性之间的等价关系。3. 证明卖空机制等价于生成势的最大凹延拓为负。4. 引入重心缩放变换(barycentric scaling transformation)将长期-only生成函数映射到受限区域以构建可控卖空策略。5. 通过分析收缩投资组合识别几何相变。

Result: 在可允许设定下，伪套利完全由生成函数在市场状态空间上的凹性刻画，放松了全局域要求；卖空行为与生成势的最大凹延拓负性等价；通过重心缩放变换可系统性地构造具有可控卖空暴露的可允许策略；在合适边界条件下，可允许策略表现出长期-only核心与卖空区域的几何相变。

Conclusion: 本文为超越长期-only约束的相对套利提供了统一的几何视角，建立了卖空机制与函数几何性质之间的严格联系，并通过重心缩放变换为实际策略设计提供了构造性方法。

Abstract: While discrete-time Stochastic Portfolio Theory (SPT) provides a robust framework for market analysis, existing work on functional generation has predominantly focused on long-only portfolios defined on the entire unit simplex. This paper extends the geometric framework of functional generation to the broader class of bankruptcy-proof long-short portfolios defined on local market state spaces. We establish that, within this admissible setting, pseudo-arbitrage is fully characterized by the concavity of the generating function on the market state space, thereby relaxing the usual global domain requirement.
  A central contribution of this work is a geometric characterization of the short-selling mechanism. We prove that the presence of short selling is equivalent to the negativity of the maximal concave extension of the generating potential. This phenomenon is linked to the steepness of the logarithmic gradient as the market approaches a zero boundary nested inside the simplex. To systematically exploit this mechanism, we introduce the barycentric scaling transformation, a constructive methodology that maps classical long-only generating functions onto restricted domains to engineer admissible strategies with controlled short-selling exposure.
  Finally, through the analysis of specific shrunken portfolios, we identify a geometric phase transition: under suitable boundary conditions, admissible strategies exhibit a long-only core and a short-selling region in a qualitative sense (without asserting an exact partition of the state space). This provides a unified geometric perspective on relative arbitrage beyond the long-only constraint.

</details>


### [196] [Quantized Stochastic Primal-Dual Methods for Distributed Optimization under Relaxed Global Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11339)
*Susmit Sarkar,Abhinav Raghuvanshi,Kushal Chakrabarti,Mayank Baranwal*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种量化随机原始-对偶方法 q-PDGD，在有限比特通信和随机梯度设置下，证明了其在受限割线不等式和 Polyak-Lojasiewicz 不等式条件下能达到与集中式最优匹配的收敛速率，并通过实验验证了量化水平、步长与图结构之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式优化方法在有限比特通信（量化梯度）和随机梯度噪声并存下的理论分析尚不充分，特别是缺乏松弛全局几何条件下的收敛保证。

Method: 提出 q-PDGD（量化随机原始-对偶梯度下降方法），在随机（无偏）量化模型下，结合原始-对偶框架进行分布式优化，并分别在受限割线不等式（RSI）和 Polyak-Lojasiewicz（PL）不等式条件下进行理论分析。

Result: 在RSI条件下，常步长实现线性收敛到由梯度噪声、量化失真和网络连通性决定的显式邻域；衰减步长达到 O(1/k) 收敛率且无需共享极小点假设。在PL条件下同样获得线性到邻域的收敛。复杂度与集中式最优随机方法匹配。

Conclusion: q-PDGD 在量化通信和随机梯度场景下具有理论保证，实验结果验证了量化级别、步长选择与图结构之间的预测权衡。

Abstract: We study distributed optimization with stochastic gradients and finite-bit communication modeled by random (unbiased) quantization. We propose q-PDGD, a quantized stochastic primal-dual method, and analyze it under relaxed global geometry. Under restricted secant inequality (RSI), a constant step-size yields linear contraction to an explicit neighborhood determined by gradient noise, quantization distortion, and network connectivity, while a diminishing step-size achieves O(1/k) convergence without shared-minimizer assumptions. Under Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL) inequality, we obtain linear-to-neighborhood convergence in the same stochastic quantized setting. Our results match the best-known centralized stochastic rates in oracle complexity, and are supported by experiments demonstrating the predicted tradeoffs between quantization level, step-size choice, and graph structure.

</details>


### [197] [Sharpness characterizes Hill functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11426)
*Marc Stephan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文严格证明：对于满足特定系数条件的n阶有理函数，其半对数尺度下的最大导数（锐度）不超过n/4，且等号成立当且仅当该函数为Hill系数为n的Hill函数，从而给出了Hill函数作为输入-输出响应锐度通用Hopfield屏障的严格数学证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Martinez-Corral等人基于Hill系数4和6的数值结果，提出Hill函数是输入-输出响应锐度的通用Hopfield屏障（即信息处理基本极限），但缺乏严格的数学证明。

Method: 对满足0≤α_i≤β_i实系数的有理函数r(x)，以半对数尺度下导数的上确界定义锐度，通过数学分析证明该锐度上界为n/4，并确定等号成立条件。

Result: 任意满足条件的n阶有理函数的锐度至多为n/4，当且仅当该函数是Hill系数为n的Hill函数时达到该上界。

Conclusion: 严格证明了Hill函数在热力学无能耗条件下可实现的最大响应锐度极限，确立了Hill函数作为通用Hopfield屏障的数学基础。

Abstract: While long treated as empirical fits, Hill functions have been postulated to be the universal Hopfield barrier for sharpness of input-output responses by Martinez-Corral, Nam, DePace, and Gunawardena. A Hopfield barrier is a fundamental limit on how well biological systems can process information without expending energy. Their case rested on numerical findings for Hill coefficients $4$ and $6$. We give a precise formulation and proof of this: measuring sharpness by the supremum of the derivative in semi-log scale, any rational function $r(x)=(α_0+α_1 x+ \cdots +α_n x^n)/(β_0 + β_1 x+ \cdots + β_n x^n)$ with real coefficients $0\leq α_i\leq β_i$ has sharpness at most $n/4$, with equality if and only if $r$ is a Hill function with Hill coefficient $n$.

</details>


### [198] [Mean field games with terminal state constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11493)
*Luciano Campi,Luca Di Persio,Viktorya Vardanyan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有状态和终端约束的均值场博弈，通过条件McKean-Vlasov型FBSDE刻画解，并在线性系数与零运行成本下证明FBSDE解的存在唯一性，同时建立Lasry-Lions意义下的唯一性结果，并应用于含二次相对绩效准则的最优投资问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MFG研究较少同时考虑状态-控制均值场交互、异质噪声驱动以及终端状态约束，本文旨在为该类MFG建立严格的解的存在唯一性理论框架。

Method: 首先构建等价辅助MFG问题；在固定流下导出辅助优化问题的随机最大值原理；通过条件McKean-Vlasov型FBSDE刻画MFG解；在特定线性系数与零运行成本情形下证明FBSDE解的存在唯一性；利用Lasry-Lions单调性方法建立唯一性。

Result: 建立了通过条件McKean-Vlasov型FBSDE刻画MFG解的表征；在线性系数和零运行成本下证明了FBSDE解的存在唯一性；获得了Lasry-Lions意义下的唯一性结果。

Conclusion: 本文为带终端约束的均值场博弈提供了基于FBSDE的严格理论框架，并将结果成功应用于含二次相对绩效准则的最优投资MFG问题。

Abstract: We study a mean field game (MFG) of state and control with state dynamics described by stochastic differential equations driven by both idiosyncratic and common noise, and subject to the constraint that the terminal state variable belongs to a nonempty, convex, closed set. The mean-field interaction enters both the dynamics and the costs through state and control. We formulate an equivalent auxiliary MFG problem and derive the stochastic maximum principle for an auxiliary optimization problem under fixed flows. By means of a suitable forward-backward stochastic differential equation (FBSDE) of conditional McKean-Vlasov type, we establish a characterization of MFG solutions via such a system. Moreover, we prove the existence and uniqueness of solutions for the FBSDE system in the specific case where the running cost is zero and the state coefficients are linear. Additionally, we obtain uniqueness results in the sense of Lasry-Lions and we apply our findings to MFGs of optimal investment with a quadratic relative performance criterion.

</details>


### [199] [Epistemic fair division of independence structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11494)
*Marcin Anholcer,Maciej Bartkowiak,Bartłomiej Bosek,Jarosław Grytczuk*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在独立集约束（如网络中的无环边集）下不可分割物品的公平分配问题。当所有代理人对物品有相同估值时，只要代理人数量至少等于图的树状度，就存在EF1分配。当代理人有任意加性估值时，存在认知EF1分配。这些结论基于抽象独立结构的通用结果，并与拟阵猜想相关。文章还提供了两个显式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际场景中（如物流、金融或社交网络），待分配的物品（如网络连接）受到结构约束——每个代理人只能接收满足特定独立性的子集（如无环边集）。现有公平分配研究大多假设无约束，而本文旨在探索在独立性约束下是否仍能实现公平分配。

Method: 问题设定为：物品集受独立集族（对子集封闭）约束，代理人必须分配到独立集。考虑两类情形：相同估值和任意加性估值。利用图论（树状度）、拟阵理论（特别是哈密顿拟阵）和组合论证，建立公平分配的存在性定理。提供了两种显式构造算法。

Result: 1) 相同估值下，若代理人数量 ≥ 图的树状度，则存在EF1分配。2) 任意加性估值下，存在认知EF1分配（即每个代理人收到的独立集存在一种剩余物品的可行分配使其不嫉妒至多一个物品）。3) 对于哈密顿拟阵，若可划分为两个独立集，则存在关于共同单调估值的EF1二分划分。4) 给出了两个显式计算算法及实例演示。

Conclusion: 在独立集约束下，公平分配（EF1/认知EF1）仍然可以实现，且关键参数是图/结构的树状度。该工作建立了公平分配与拟阵理论之间的联系，为结构约束下的资源分配提供了理论基础和算法工具。

Abstract: We study the problem of fair division of indivisible goods with constraints imposed by a prescribed independence structure, that is, a family of subsets of goods closed under taking subsets. As a motivating example, imagine that the goods to be divided are the available connections in a logistic, financial, or social network. The admissible bundle of goods for each agent must correspond to an acyclic set of edges, corresponding to a basic feasible solution to a linear network problem to be solved. Suppose that all agents assign the same value to each good (in the example, the network connections are equally important for every agent) and evaluate each bundle by summing the values of its goods. Is there a fair partition of the goods into such acyclic bundles?
  Surprisingly, the answer is yes, provided that the number of agents is at least the arboricity of $G$, and the fairness requirement is envy-freeness up to one good (EF1). The situation becomes more mysterious when agents have arbitrary additive valuations. Our main result guarantees that, in this case, epistemic EF1 partitions always exist, which means that each agent receives an acyclic bundle for which there exists a feasible partition of the remaining goods into acyclic bundles that they do not envy up to one good.
  We derive this conclusion from a general result for abstract independence structures defined on the sets of goods. We also discuss connections with several conjectures concerning matroids. In particular, we prove that any Hamiltonian matroid partitionable into two independent sets admits an EF1 bipartition with respect to a common monotone valuation.
  We complement our results with a constructive perspective: we present explicitly two algorithms for computing the fair allocations described above. Finally, we provide illustrative examples to demonstrate these algorithms on specific instances.

</details>


### [200] [Nonlocal Onsager Operators and Entropy Dissipation for Finite-State Schrödinger Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11513)
*Abdallah BenAbdallah,Mohsen Dlal*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对有限状态空间上的薛定谔桥问题，提出了一种连续时间演化动力学（SBOF），证明了其平衡态等价于桥问题的唯一解，并建立了非局部Onsager算子、Dirichlet形式及相对熵的梯度流解释，在正性假设下证明了全局适定性、收敛性和指数松弛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薛定谔桥问题在最优传输、随机过程和生成建模中具有重要地位，但现有方法缺乏直接的连续时间动力学解释，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 从半对偶凸形式出发，引入终端薛定谔势的连续时间演化；推导终端边际演化方程，识别出状态依赖的非局部Onsager算子（半对偶泛函的Hessian）；导出Dirichlet形式并在适当商空间上建立强制性估计；利用一致Poincaré不等式和熵-方差比较估计。

Result: 在正性假设下证明了SBOF的全局适定性、收敛到薛定谔桥、耦合与路径测度的收敛性，以及终端边际的指数松弛速率。

Conclusion: 薛定谔桥问题可被解释为非局部相对熵梯度流，该动力学框架可应用于有限状态生成建模（通过Doob变换），并在含稀有状态的网格示例上得到验证。

Abstract: We investigate the Schrödinger bridge problem on a finite state space with a strictly positive Markov reference kernel. Starting from the semi-dual convex formulation, we introduce a continuous-time evolution for the terminal Schrödinger potential and show that its equilibria coincide with the unique solution of the bridge problem.
  The proposed dynamics induces an evolution for the terminal marginal. This marginal equation is governed by a state-dependent nonlocal Onsager operator, identified with the Hessian of the semi-dual functional. We derive its associated Dirichlet form, establish coercivity estimates on the appropriate quotient space, and interpret the resulting equation as a nonlocal gradient-flow formulation of relative entropy.
  Under natural positivity assumptions, we prove global well-posedness of the SBOF, convergence to the Schrödinger bridge, convergence of the induced couplings and path measures, and exponential relaxation of the terminal marginal. The latter follows from a uniform Poincaré inequality on compact sublevel sets together with entropy--variance comparison estimates. We also discuss the connection with finite-state generative modeling through the Doob transform and illustrate the theory on finite-grid examples involving rare states.

</details>


### [201] [Exponential Adaptive Smoothing and Importance Sampling for Optimization of the Conditional Value-at-Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11515)
*Will Asness,Brendan Keith,Boyan Lazarov,Anton Malandii,Stan Uryasev*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于CVaR对偶表示的Bregman近端点算法，通过交替随机原始-对偶阶段求解条件风险价值优化问题。对偶分布的似然比收敛到解的CVaR风险标识符，实现内置重要性采样机制，显著优于其他随机逼近方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机逼近方法在求解CVaR优化问题时效率低下，因为CVaR仅关注分布的尾部样本，而大量非尾部样本的计算浪费了计算资源。需要一种能够自动聚焦于尾部样本的高效算法。

Method: 基于CVaR的对偶表示（worst-case期望），采用Bregman近端点算法，交替执行随机原始阶段（求解子问题）和对偶阶段（更新概率分布）。对偶分布相对于原始分布的似然比收敛到解的CVaR风险标识符，形成内置重要性采样机制。

Result: 对于凸目标函数证明了算法收敛性。在金融数学（投资组合优化）和机器学习（支持向量机）的数值实验中表现出卓越性能，显著优于其他随机逼近方法。

Conclusion: 所提算法通过内置重要性采样机制自动聚焦于分布尾部样本，大幅提高了CVaR优化问题的求解效率，在金融和机器学习领域具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: We present a novel method for solving conditional value-at-risk (CVaR) optimization problems based on the dual representation of CVaR, which is defined as the worst-case expectation over a risk envelope. The method is based on the Bregman proximal point algorithm and alternates between stochastic primal and dual stages. Every (inner) primal stage involves a subproblem solved by sampling from a probability distribution updated at each dual stage (outer iteration). The likelihood ratio of the dual probability distributions relative to the distribution underlying the original problem converges to the risk identifier of the solution's CVaR. Thus, the dual distribution provides the algorithm with a built-in importance sampling mechanism that draws from the tail of the underlying distribution. Because only samples in the tail influence the CVaR, and samples outside the tail are drawn with decreasing probability, the algorithm delivers exceptional performance over other stochastic approximation methods. We prove the convergence of the algorithm for convex objective functions. Our numerical experiments target representative problems in financial mathematics and machine learning, focusing on portfolio optimization and support-vector machines, respectively.

</details>


### [202] [Convex Generalized Differentiation at infinity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11538)
*Nguyen Xuan Duy Bao,Nguyen Mau Nam*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文发展了凸集和凸函数在无穷远处的广义微分理论，研究了无穷远处的切锥、法锥和次微分等基本概念，在凸性假设下建立了更弱条件、更简洁表示的结果，并应用于无界可行集的凸优化问题的最优性条件和可达性准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥补现有非光滑分析在无穷远处理论在凸性设定下的不足，利用凸性使得构造保持凸性并与衰退分析、对偶性和上图几何建立自然联系，从而在更弱假设下获得更简洁显式的表示。

Method: 基于凸分析框架，系统研究无穷远处切锥、法锥和次微分的定义与性质，发展相应的微积分法则和几何刻画，并与衰退分析、极性理论建立联系。

Result: 在凸性条件下，许多结果可在更弱假设下建立，且具有更简单显式的表示；发展了相应的微积分法则和几何刻画；成功应用于无界可行集凸优化问题的最优性条件和可达性准则。

Conclusion: 为凸集和凸函数在无穷远处的分析提供了新工具，进一步强化了凸分析、变分分析与优化之间的理论联系。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a generalized differentiation theory at infinity for convex sets and functions. In particular, we study several fundamental notions of convex analysis at infinity, including tangent cones, normal cones, and subdifferentials. Our work complements the recently developed theories of nonsmooth analysis at infinity by focusing on the convex setting, where these constructions preserve convexity and admit natural connections with recession analysis, polarity, and epigraphical geometry. We show that, in the convex case, many results can be established under weaker assumptions and admit simpler and more explicit representations than those available in the general nonsmooth framework. In addition, we develop calculus rules and geometric characterizations for these constructions and apply them to optimality conditions and attainment criteria for convex optimization problems over unbounded feasible sets. The results obtained in this paper provide new tools for the study of convex sets and functions at infinity and further strengthen the connections between convex analysis, variational analysis, and optimization.

</details>


### [203] [Last-Iterate Convergence of Optimistic Multiplicative Weight Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11773)
*Francesco Orabona*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了OMWU算法在光滑凸凹鞍点问题中，使用足够小的常数学习率时能够渐近收敛，且不需要唯一性、严格互补、误差界或接近解的初始化条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OGDA（欧几里得版本）的末次迭代在光滑问题中渐近收敛到鞍点是80年代已知的结果，但OMWU（非欧几里得、熵版本）是否具有相同性质此前未知。

Method: 引入了一个边界论证（boundary argument），证明每个聚点都满足非活跃坐标的KKT不等式。该论证在ChatGPT辅助下发现，并在附录中记录。

Result: OMWU在光滑凸凹鞍点问题中，使用足够小的常数学习率时渐近收敛。

Conclusion: 填补了OMWU收敛理论的一个空白，表明该算法的收敛性质与OGDA一致，无需额外假设条件。

Abstract: Optimistic Gradient Descent Ascent (OGDA) and Optimistic Multiplicative-Weights Update (OMWU) are two very popular algorithms to solve convex/concave saddle-point problems, where OMWU is the non-Euclidean, entropic version of OGDA. It is known since the '80s that the last iterate of OGDA asymptotically converges to a saddle point in smooth problems. On the other hand, it is unknown if OMWU has the same property. In this paper, I show that OMWU converges asymptotically for smooth convex-concave saddle-point problems, with a small enough constant learning rate. The result does not require uniqueness, strict complementarity, an error bound, or initialization near a solution. The main new ingredient is a boundary argument showing that every cluster point satisfies the inactive-coordinate KKT inequalities. The boundary argument was discovered with assistance from ChatGPT and is documented in the appendix.

</details>


### [204] [bAdag: an adaptive block coordinate gradient method for smooth nonconvex functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11791)
*Giovanni Seraghiti*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为bAdag的块坐标梯度方法，基于AdaGrad算法，属于无目标函数优化方法，用于求解光滑非凸最小化问题。该方法使用块梯度的累积和计算自适应步长，支持三种块选择策略（循环、均匀随机、贪心高斯-南威尔），并证明了次线性收敛速度，同时扩展到带箱约束的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AdaGrad类方法依赖全梯度计算，计算开销大；块坐标方法虽能降低单次迭代成本，但缺乏自适应步长机制。本文旨在融合块坐标下降与自适应梯度方法的优势，提出一种高效且具有理论保证的无目标函数优化方法。

Method: 基于AdaGrad算法，用块梯度的累积和替代全梯度累积和来设计自适应步长；支持循环、均匀随机和高斯-南威尔三种块选择策略；在块Lipschitz连续假设下分析收敛性，并推广到带箱约束的平滑问题。

Result: 证明了bAdag在光滑非凸目标函数下的遍历次线性收敛速度；三种块选择策略均具有理论保证；通过合成数据和真实世界实验验证了算法有效性。

Conclusion: bAdag成功将AdaGrad的自适应步长思想融入块坐标框架，兼顾了计算效率与收敛保证，为大规模非凸优化提供了新的实用工具。

Abstract: A new Block Coordinate Gradient (BCG) method, dubbed bAdag, for smooth, nonconvex minimization problem is proposed; it falls in the class of Objective Function Free Optimization (OFFO) methods, and it is based on the AdaGrad algorithm. At each iteration, our method computes an adaptive step size based on the cumulative sum of block gradients, instead of full gradients as in AdaGrad-type methods. We prove ergodic, sublinear convergence rates for the bAdag algorithm when minimizing a smooth, possibly nonconvex objective under the (block) Lipschitz continuity assumption on the gradient. Our theory covers three widely popular block selection strategies: the Cyclic (C) rule, Uniform Random selection (UR), and the greedy Gauss-Southwell (GS) rule. We also extend our algorithm and its convergence theory to box-constrained smooth functions. We validate the proposed algorithms through synthetic and real-world experiments.

</details>


### [205] [Accelerated Implicit GDA Schemes: Theoretical Guarantees and Application to Proximal Augmented Lagrangian Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11800)
*Jiaqi Liu,Bin Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出将近端操作引入增广拉格朗日框架得到近端ALM，证明其外迭代等价于隐式GDA，并基于连续时间ODE框架发展了一系列隐式和显式GDA方法，实现了对原始-对偶目标间隙和梯度范数的最后迭代收敛率加速（o(1/k^{r+1})），并通过数值实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带有线性等式约束的凸优化问题广泛出现在科学计算、机器学习和控制理论中。经典Krylov方法依赖特定问题的预处理器且内存消耗大，而梯度类方法（如增广拉格朗日方法ALM）虽避免了这些问题但外迭代收敛缓慢。因此，开发加速的外迭代方案是一个关键研究目标。

Method: 1) 将近端操作引入增广拉格朗日框架得到近端ALM，证明其外迭代等价于隐式梯度下降-上升（GDA）方案，并扩展到变步长设置；2) 通过Lyapunov分析揭示势函数应从传统目标间隙转变为变分不等式度量；3) 基于连续时间ODE框架提出隐式GDA变步长方案，实现o(1/k)最后迭代收敛率；4) 基于二阶ODE框架提出参数化r≥0的Nesterov型隐式GDA方案族，实现o(1/k^{r+1})最后迭代收敛率；5) 将二阶ODE特例化为r=0，推导出显式GDA方案并证明o(1/k)最后迭代收敛率。

Result: 隐式GDA变步长方案实现了对原始-对偶目标间隙和梯度范数的o(1/k)最后迭代收敛率；Nesterov型隐式GDA方案族（参数r≥0）实现了对原始-对偶目标间隙的o(1/k^{r+1})最后迭代收敛率；显式GDA方案（r=0特例）实现了o(1/k)最后迭代收敛率。数值实验验证了理论结果和方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过建立近端ALM与隐式GDA的等价性，以及从纯凸优化到极小极大优化的视角转变，本文成功开发了一系列加速的外迭代方案，在理论上实现了更优的最后迭代收敛率保证，为求解带有线性等式约束的凸优化问题提供了新的高效方法。

Abstract: Convex optimization problems with linear equality constraints arise ubiquitously in scientific computing, machine learning, and control theory. Classical Krylov methods are effective but rely on problem-specific preconditioners and high memory. Conversely, gradient-based methods like the augmented Lagrangian method (ALM) avoid these issues yet suffer from slow outer iterations. Developing accelerated outer-iteration schemes, therefore, remains a critical research objective. In this study, we demonstrate that incorporating a proximal operation into the augmented Lagrangian framework yields the proximal ALM, where the outer iteration is equivalent to an implicit gradient descent-ascent (GDA) scheme. We further establish that this equivalence extends naturally to the setting of variable step sizes. Through Lyapunov analysis, we show that the underlying potential function must be shifted from the conventional objective gap to a variational inequality measure, signaling a shift in perspective from pure convex optimization to minimax optimization. Motivated by these observations, we first develop an implicit GDA scheme with variable step sizes based on a continuous-time ODE framework, which achieves an $o(1/k)$ last-iterate convergence rate for both the primal-dual objective gap and the gradient norm. Building upon a second-order ODE framework, we then propose a family of Nesterov-type implicit GDA schemes parameterized by $r \geq 0$, which achieves an $o(1/k^{r+1})$ last-iterate convergence rate for the primal-dual objective gap. Furthermore, specializing the second-order ODE formulation to the case $r=0$, we derive a corresponding explicit GDA scheme and prove an $o(1/k)$ last-iterate convergence rate for the primal-dual objective gap. Finally, we present several numerical experiments to validate these theoretical results and demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [206] [On finding exact solutions of linear programs in the oracle model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11820)
*Daniel Dadush,László A. Végh,Giacomo Zambelli*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在线性规划黑箱模型（分离预言机）中求解精确原始-对偶解的算法，该算法在多项式时间内完成，其复杂度由几何条件数δ决定，不依赖于目标向量c，且无需预先知道δ。算法利用近似求解器（切割平面法）结合Burrell-Todd框架提取对偶证书，避免了传统基于舍入和比特复杂度的论证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线性规划的黑箱模型下，已有工作（Grötschel-Lovász-Schrijver, Frank-Tardos）依赖比特复杂度和丢番图逼近等舍入方法。本文旨在开发一种几何方法，避免舍入论证，实现精确原始-对偶解的求解，并保证多项式时间复杂性。

Method: 算法采用黑箱方式，调用近似原始-对偶求解子程序（如Jiang-Lee-Song-Wong的切割平面法）。基于Burrell和Todd（1985）的工作，构建了从近似原始解中提取精确对偶证书的通用框架。算法的复杂度分析依赖于几何条件数δ，并通过几何论证而非比特复杂度来保证收敛。

Result: 算法在分离预言机模型下，使用O(n² log(n/δ))次预言机调用和O(n⁴ log(n/δ)+n⁵ log log(1/δ))次算术运算即可找到精确的原始和对偶解。对于有理数数据，log(1/δ)被编码规模多项式有界，因此算法是多项式时间的。

Conclusion: 本文通过几何方法解决了线性规划黑箱模型中的精确求解问题，强化了Grötschel-Lovász-Schrijver和Frank-Tardos的结果，避免了传统的比特复杂度和舍入论证，为组合优化中的精确求解提供了新途径。

Abstract: We consider linear programming in the oracle model: $\max\{c^\top x \,:\, x\in P\}$, where the polyhedron $P=\{x\in\mathbb{R}^n\,:\, Ax\le b\}$ is given by a separation oracle. We present an algorithm that finds exact primal and dual solutions using $O(n^2\log(n/δ))$ oracle calls and $O(n^4\log(n/δ)+n^5\log\log(1/δ))$ arithmetic operations, where $δ$ is a geometric condition number associated with the system $(A,b)$. These bounds do not depend on the cost vector $c$ and do not require a priori knowledge of $δ$. For rational data, $\log(1/δ)$ is polynomially bounded in the encoding size of $(A,b)$, thus providing a polynomial-time algorithm.
  The algorithm works in a black box manner, requiring a subroutine for approximate primal and dual solutions; the above running times are achieved when using the cutting plane method of Jiang, Lee, Song, and Wong (STOC 2020) for this subroutine. Whereas approximate solvers may return primal solutions only, we develop a general framework for extracting dual certificates based on the work of Burrell and Todd (Math. Oper. Res. 1985).
  Our algorithm strengthens results by Grötschel, Lovász, and Schrijver (Prog. Comb. Opt. 1984), and by Frank and Tardos (Combinatorica 1987) that rely on bit-complexity arguments. Our algorithm avoids rounding-based arguments such as simultaneous Diophantine approximation and uses geometric arguments instead.

</details>


### [207] [Distributionally Robust Reinsurance under Robust Optimized Certainty Equivalent Risk Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11855)
*Xinqiao Xie,Taizhong Hu,Tiantian Mao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类偏好稳健风险度量——鲁棒优化确定性等价（ROCE），涵盖条件风险价值（CVaR）和期望分位数（expectiles）等常用度量作为特例。基于分布鲁棒最优再保险（DROR）的最新进展，研究了均值-方差不确定集和Wasserstein不确定集下的DROR问题。对均值-方差不确定集，通过证明只需考虑三点分布，将无穷维优化问题转化为有限维问题，得到统一显式公式；对Wasserstein不确定集也得到了可处理的有限维形式。数值实验展示了所提方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风险度量在分布鲁棒优化框架下缺乏统一处理，且不同不确定集（矩型与Wasserstein型）在最优再保险设计中的系统比较尚未充分研究。引入ROCE风险度量旨在统一CVaR和expectiles等多种风险度量，并建立可计算的分布鲁棒再保险优化框架。

Method: 提出ROCE风险度量类，在分布鲁棒最优再保险问题中分别采用均值-方差不确定集和Wasserstein不确定集。对于均值-方差不确定集，利用三点分布重构将无穷维优化问题简化为有限维问题，导出统一显式解；对于Wasserstein不确定集，同样推导出有限维可计算形式。通过数值实验验证模型有效性。

Result: 均值-方差不确定集下，ROCE框架成功将问题降维至有限维，统一了CVaR和expectiles的特例结果；Wasserstein不确定集下也得到了可处理的有限维公式。数值实验验证了所提方法的计算效率和两种不确定集在最优免赔额设计上的系统对比。

Conclusion: ROCE为分布鲁棒再保险问题提供了统一且可计算的框架，桥接了矩型与Wasserstein型不确定集下的优化方法，为实际风险管理中的风险度量选择和不确定集比较提供了理论支撑与计算工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a class of preference robust risk measures-\emph{robust optimized certainty equivalents} (ROCE)-which encompasses several widely used measures, including Conditional Value-at-Risk and expectiles, as special cases. Motivated by recent developments in distributionally robust optimal reinsurance (DROR), we investigate DROR problems under the ROCE risk measure and consider two prominent uncertainty sets: the mean-variance uncertainty set and the Wasserstein uncertainty set. For the mean-variance uncertainty set, we reformulate the infinite-dimensional optimization problem into a finite-dimensional one by showing that it suffices to consider three-point distributions. This leads to a unified and explicit formulation for a broad class of ROCE risk measures and offers a simplified framework that also recovers earlier results for Conditional Value-at-Risk and expectiles. For the Wasserstein uncertainty set, we also derive a tractable finite-dimensional formulation. The resulting data-driven models enable efficient computation and facilitate a systematic comparison between moment-based and Wasserstein-based uncertainty sets in the optimal deductible design. Numerical experiments are exhibited to illustrate the performance of our reformulated programs.

</details>


### [208] [Masked Symmetric Nonnegative Matrix Factorization for Community Detection in Incomplete Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11981)
*Anqi Liu,Ran Gu,Rui-Jin Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出Masked SymNMF框架，通过掩码操作直接分解部分观测网络，并采用非对称松弛加正则化项解决非凸优化问题，在合成与真实网络上优于传统零填充方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对称非负矩阵分解在处理不完整邻接矩阵时依赖零填充，这降低了聚类可靠性，需要一种直接处理部分观测网络的方法。

Method: 提出掩码对称非负矩阵分解框架，在观测项上定义掩码算子；引入非对称松弛并加正则化项，证明精确罚性质；开发交替非负最小二乘框架，推导出乘法更新、分层交替最小二乘和投影梯度下降的更新规则。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实网络上的大量数值实验表明，Masked SymNMF在不同观测密度下均优于基线填充方法。

Conclusion: 该方法为不完整网络中的社区检测提供了一种理论可靠且实践高效的方案。

Abstract: Community detection in complex networks is frequently challenged by incomplete or noisy adjacency matrices. Traditional symmetric nonnegative matrix factorization methods typically rely on zero-imputation for unobserved entries, which compromises clustering reliability. This paper proposes a Masked Symmetric Nonnegative Matrix Factorization (Masked SymNMF) framework designed to factorize partially observed networks directly. By defining a masking operator over the observed entries, the proposed model restricts the objective evaluation exclusively to valid data. To overcome the severe non-convexity inherent in the symmetric factorization, we formulate an asymmetric relaxation penalized by a regularization term. We prove the exact penalty property of this reformulated model, establishing its theoretical equivalence to the original symmetric problem under sufficient regularization. Furthermore, an alternating nonnegative least squares framework is developed, yielding tailored update rules for Multiplicative Updates, Hierarchical Alternating Least Squares, and Projected Gradient Descent algorithms. Extensive numerical experiments on synthetic datasets and real-world networks demonstrate that the proposed Masked SymNMF outperforms baseline imputation methods across varying observation densities, providing a theoretically sound and practically efficient approach for community detection in incomplete networks.

</details>


### [209] [Constrained Lyapunov Stabilization based on Gauss Variational Equations: From Spacecraft Orbital Transfers to Rendezvous](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12108)
*Ilya Kolmanovsky,Emanuele Garone,Grant Touchette*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文基于高斯变分方程（GVE）构建Lyapunov反馈律，通过障碍函数和饱和函数处理状态/控制约束，并引入参考调节器避免伪平衡点。将方法扩展到交会机动，使所有六个轨道元素（包括时变真近点角）匹配目标值，通过外环Lyapunov反馈调整半长轴。仿真验证了常规推进和洛伦兹力推进（仅控制系绳电流）两种场景下的安全闭环交会机动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将已有的基于GVE的Lyapunov反馈律（仅匹配五个轨道元素）扩展到完整的六元素交会机动，解决真近点角时变目标的跟踪问题，并同时满足状态和控制约束。

Method: 利用障碍函数强制执行状态约束，饱和函数处理控制约束，并采用参考调节器作为收敛调节器避免障碍函数产生的伪平衡点。针对交会问题，增加外环Lyapunov反馈律修改半长轴指令以匹配时变真近点角。

Result: 通过常规推进和洛伦兹力推进（仅控制系绳电流并受电流限制）两种推进方式的仿真，验证了所提安全闭环交会机动方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的Lyapunov反馈律能够成功实现满足多约束条件的轨道交会机动，适用于不同推进方式（传统推进和洛伦兹力推进），具有较好的通用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Lyapunov feedback laws can be constructed for performing orbital transfer maneuvers based on Gauss Variational Equations, or GVEs, while satisfying specified state and control constraints. These state and control constraints are enforced using barrier functions and saturation, respectively, while the reference governor, employed as a convergence governor, is used to avoid getting stuck at spurious equilibria that may be created by barrier functions. In this article, these Lyapunov feedback laws are extended to rendezvous maneuvers where not only five orbital elements are matched to prescribed target values, but also the true anomaly matches its time dependent target value. The modification involves altering the commanded semi major axis with an outer loop feedback law, also designed using Lyapunov techniques. We illustrate the resulting safe closed loop rendezvous maneuvers in simulations for conventional thrust based propulsion and Lorentz force-based propulsion. In the latter case, only the current through the tether is controlled, subject to current limits, to accomplish prescribed orbital transfer and rendezvous maneuvers.

</details>


### [210] [A Unified Zeroth-Order Approach for Decentralized Minimax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12124)
*Haoyuan Cai,Yike Zhao,Aleksandar Armacki,Jie Chen,Ali H. Sayed*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: ZOMA是一个统一的零阶分布式加速最小最大框架，适用于多智能体非凸Polyak--Łojasiewicz最小最大优化。该框架仅需评估函数值，在梯度不可用或计算昂贵的场景中具有优势。核心贡献是多层次统一：混合零阶估计器、多种偏差校正策略（GT、ED、EXTRA）及多种加速技术（STORM、PAGE、L2S的零阶版本），建立了统一的收敛保证，匹配最先进集中式方法的性能，并实现用户数量的线性加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决梯度不可用或计算成本高昂环境下多智能体非凸PL最小最大优化的分布式零阶方法缺失问题，以及现有方法在估计器、偏差校正和加速技术上的碎片化现状，需要一个统一框架来系统化地比较和设计算法。

Method: 提出ZOMA框架，采用混合零阶估计器（支持坐标式和随机均匀平滑估计器），集成多种偏差校正策略（梯度跟踪GT、精确扩散ED、EXTRA），并融入多种加速技术（STORM、PAGE、L2S的零阶版本），从而在分布式无梯度环境下实现非凸PL最小最大优化。

Result: 建立了统一收敛保证，匹配最先进的集中式零阶最小最大方法的性能，并实现用户数量的线性加速。通过数值模拟验证了算法性能。

Conclusion: ZOMA框架通过多层次统一，为分布式零阶最小最大优化提供了系统性设计与分析方法，具备通用性和高效性，可针对具体问题结构和算法设计专门化收敛率。

Abstract: We propose ZOMA, a unified Zeroth-Order decentralized accelerated MinimAx framework for multi-agent nonconvex Polyak--Łojasiewicz minimax optimization. The proposed framework only requires evaluating the function value and, as such, is tailored to gradient-free environments, where exact gradient information is either unavailable or computationally prohibitive to obtain. A central contribution of our \textbf{ZOMA} framework is a multi-level unification, along the following directions: (i) \emph{estimator} - our framework adopts a hybrid zeroth-order estimator, which accommodates, among others, both coordinate-wise and randomized uniform smoothing estimators;
  (ii) \emph{bias correction} - our framework subsumes a wide range of bias-correction strategies, including gradient tracking (GT), exact diffusion (ED), and EXTRA and (iii) \emph{acceleration} - our framework facilitates a broad class of acceleration techniques, including zeroth-order versions of STORM, PAGE, and L2S. The general nature of \textbf{ZOMA} leads to many novel decentralized zeroth-order minimax methods and allows us to establish unified convergence guarantees, matching the performance of state-of-the-art centralized zeroth-order minimax methods, while providing benefits, such as linear speed-up in the number of users. The unified framework also provides a systematic way to assess algorithmic suitability by specializing the convergence rates to specific problem structures and method designs. We validate the performance of the proposed algorithms via numerical simulations.

</details>


### [211] [Kernel-based identification of nonlinear port-Hamiltonian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12163)
*Brayan M. Shali,Henk J. van Waarde*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于核方法的端口-哈密顿系统辨识框架，通过表示定理将无限维优化问题转化为有限维问题，并给出了非凸问题的求解算法及其收敛性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端口-哈密顿系统建模通常需要详细的物理洞察和精确的系统参数，实践中往往难以获得，因此需要一种能从输入-状态-输出数据中直接辨识系统模型的数据驱动方法。

Method: 将定义端口-哈密顿系统的映射表示在再生核希尔伯特空间中，构建无限维优化问题，利用表示定理将其简化为有限维优化问题，并针对简化后的非凸问题设计迭代算法并证明其收敛性。

Result: 建立了表示定理，将无限维函数空间上的优化问题转化为有限维参数优化问题；给出了非凸优化求解算法并证明了算法的收敛性。

Conclusion: 所提出的核方法框架能够有效从数据中辨识端口-哈密顿系统，无需显式的物理建模，且通过表示定理保证了计算可行性。

Abstract: Port-Hamiltonian systems provide a structured framework for modeling physical systems by explicitly capturing their energy storage, dissipation, and exchange. However, deriving such models often requires detailed physical insight and precise knowledge of system parameters, which may not be available in practice. In this paper, we propose a kernel-based framework for the identification of port-Hamiltonian systems from input-state-output data. In contrast to conventional parametric approaches, the maps defining the port-Hamiltonian system are represented in suitably chosen reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces. This leads to an infinite-dimensional optimization problem over the corresponding function spaces. Our main result establishes a representer theorem that reduces this problem to a tractable finite-dimensional one. Since the reduced problem is non-convex, we further provide an algorithm for its solution and prove its convergence.

</details>


### [212] [Pricing mobility services under decision-dependent demand uncertainty: a carsharing case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12165)
*Jiali Deng,Giovanni Pantuso*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究移动服务定价问题，构建了决策依赖需求不确定性的随机规划模型，通过改进的L-shaped方法求解，在真实汽车共享系统案例中相比传统方法提升预期利润约8.4%-8.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多将不确定需求视为已知分布的外生随机变量，忽略了价格对用户采纳决策的影响，需要更准确地建模定价与需求之间的依赖关系。

Method: 将定价问题建模为具有决策依赖需求不确定性的随机规划，转化为混合整数线性规划；专门设计了L-shaped方法，推导了子问题的闭式原始和对偶解以实现高效分离，并开发了问题特定的有效不等式和割共享机制以加速收敛。

Result: 在真实汽车共享系统案例研究中，相比确定性价格弹性需求基准提升预期利润8.39%，相比外生随机需求基准提升8.53%；提出的方法在求解性能上远优于商业求解器。

Conclusion: 纳入决策依赖的不确定性对定价决策至关重要；受控的车辆分配策略能在仅轻微影响利润的同时显著提升服务率。

Abstract: The problem of pricing mobility services has attracted significant attention. In most studies, uncertain demand is modeled as an exogenous random variable with known distribution. This assumption overlooks the likely effect of prices on user adoption decisions. To address this dependency, we formulate the pricing problem as a stochastic program with decision-dependent demand uncertainty. Specifically, we make the non-standard assumption that the probability distribution of demand depends on pricing decisions. We show that the problem can be written as a mixed-integer linear program whose size is exponential in the input parameters. To find exact numerical solutions we specialize the L-shaped method for stochastic programs with decision-dependent uncertainty. In particular, we devise efficient separation routines by proving closed-form primal and dual solutions to the involved subproblems. In addition, we develop problem-specific valid inequalities and cut-sharing mechanisms which significantly improve convergence. We show that the method outperforms by far a commercial solver used to solve the monolithic formulation. Furthermore, in a case study based on a real-world carsharing system, we show that incorporating decision-dependent uncertainty improves expected profits by 8.39% compared to a benchmark that considers deterministic price-elastic demand, and by 8.53% compared to a benchmark that considers exogenous random demand, on average. In addition, we evaluate the performance of preventive pricing and relocation decisions under two vehicle allocation policies. The results suggest that a controlled allocation of vehicles to customers can improve service rates while only marginally affecting profits.

</details>


### [213] [LPV Updates for Sequentially Linearized Moving Horizon Estimation of Nonlinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12177)
*Jiaxin Ji,Jan Heiland,Dimitrios S. Karachalios,Hossam S. Abbas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于线性变参数（LPV）公式的高效 moving horizon estimation（MHE）方案，通过将调度参数设置为系统状态估计值并构建非精确雅可比矩阵，将非线性优化问题转化为二次规划（QP）子问题，从而降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MHE在每个采样步需要求解非线性优化问题，计算负担大，限制了其在实际系统中的应用。本文旨在通过LPV公式降低MHE的计算复杂度。

Method: 基于LPV公式构建MHE方案，调度参数由系统状态估计得到，用于构造非精确雅可比矩阵。雅可比矩阵可离线预先指定为结构化形式，在QP子问题中在线更新，避免标准非线性规划（NLP）的高计算成本。

Result: 通过数值仿真验证了所提方法的性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的LPV-MHE方案在保持估计精度的同时，显著降低了计算负担，具有较好的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Moving horizon estimation (MHE) provides high precision state estimation for nonlinear systems, but it is often limited by the substantial computational demands of solving a nonlinear optimization problem at every sampling step. To address this issue, we develop an efficient MHE scheme based on linear parameter-varying (LPV) formulation, where the scheduling parameters are given by the estimated states of the system and used to construct inexact Jacobians. Due to the LPV representation, the Jacobian can be pre-specified offline in a structured form and then updated in the quadratic programming (QP) subproblem, which reduces computational cost commonly used in standard nonlinear programming (NLP) systems. We illustrate the performance by numerical simulations.

</details>


### [214] [Averaging of Random Vibrations in Mechanical Systems in the Sense of Ito, Stratonovich, and Sussmann](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12266)
*Raik Suttner,Christian Ebenbauer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类随机振动下机械系统的随机平均原理，证明了确定性平均原理可推广至非周期随机输入，并展示了随机微分方程的解可直接由常微分方程计算，最后通过非完整车辆的随机源搜索示例进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将已知的仿射连接控制系统中大振幅高频输入的确定性平均原理推广到非周期随机输入情形，建立随机振动机-械系统的平均原理。

Method: 采用不依赖于Itô、Stratonovich或Sussmann解释的随机微分方程描述随机振动机械系统，推导随机平均原理，并证明其解可由单个常微分方程直接计算。

Result: 证明了确定性平均原理在非周期随机输入下依然成立；随机微分方程的解不依赖于其解释方式，且可由常微分方程直接求得。

Conclusion: 所提出的随机平均原理为随机振动下机械系统的分析与控制提供了理论基础，非完整车辆的随机源搜索示例验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate a stochastic averaging principle for a large class of mechanical systems in the presence of random vibrations. We show that a known deterministic averaging principle for affine connection control systems with large-amplitude high-frequency inputs also holds for non-periodic stochastic inputs. The randomly vibrating mechanical system is described by a stochastic differential equation whose solutions do not depend on its interpretation in the sense of Ito, Stratonovich, or Sussmann. We also show that solutions of this stochastic differential equation can be directly computed from a single ordinary differential equation. We illustrate our theoretical results by the example of stochastic source seeking with a nonholonomic vehicle.

</details>


### [215] [An Efficient Method for the Optimal Control of Microgrids Under Uncertainties using Local Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12345)
*Edoardo Scaccia,Eric C. Kerrigan,Anna Sadowska*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并比较了微电网最优容量与功率调度问题的两种鲁棒优化形式：一种使用二元变量和大M约束的混合整数线性规划，另一种通过精确光滑重构转化为连续非线性规划。并提出了局部约简算法求解，两种方法在10万样本蒙特卡洛模拟中可行性率均超过90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微电网最优容量与功率调度问题在不确定性下常被建模为混合整数规划，但现有数值方法（如场景法）仅能近似求解，需要更精确高效的鲁棒优化方法。

Method: 1) 提出两种鲁棒优化形式：MILP形式（二元变量+大M约束）和连续NLP形式（精确光滑重构）；2) 提出局部约简算法扩展求解两种问题；3) 基于10万样本蒙特卡洛模拟进行对比评估。

Result: 两种形式在10万样本蒙特卡洛模拟中均达到90%以上可行性率，结果令人满意。

Conclusion: 提出的两种鲁棒形式及局部约简算法能有效处理含逻辑约束和不确定性的微电网优化问题，高可行性率验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: The problem of optimal sizing and power scheduling in microgrids subject to uncertainties is well known to the control community. Commonly, the optimal control problem is cast as a mixed-integer program to model the logical constraints arising in energy storage systems, and is then solved approximately using numerical methods such as the scenario approach. In this paper, we propose and compare two formulations of a robust microgrid sizing and power scheduling optimal control problem with logical constraints and uncertainties in the user's power demand, solar power generation, grid electricity prices and battery efficiencies.
